import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess

REPO = Path(os.path.expanduser("~/Desktop/FX_Project/ofilt_pass1"))

after = "--after" in sys.argv
ok = True


def chk(label, cond):
    global ok
    ok &= bool(cond)
    print(f"{label}: {bool(cond)}")

    print(f"HEAD {head} (step f: the new checkpoint; not compared with step a's value)")

"""P3-8a, the configuration check of the records rule (post-switch form), at steps a.3, e.7 and f.2. Runs
`tools/p3_sealed_run.py --check-config` and `validate_config` of the committed configuration in process. Expected values
are read by script from the saved P3-8a message's records-rule paragraph."""

import subprocess

--- b. Read-only facts for P3-8b (/tmp/p3_8a_b.py) ---

"""P3-8a step b: read-only facts for P3-8b, printed with file and line numbers, without interpretation. Reads tracked
files under tests/, ofilt/ and tools/ as text only; runs no test; opens no data file. The names of b.2, b.3 and b.7 must
each match exactly one definition (else STOP). "Enclosing" is P3-7b's rule (enclosing in /tmp/p3_7b_c.py), copied."""
import ast
import hashlib
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(os.path.expanduser("~/Desktop/FX_Project/ofilt_pass1"))
git = lambda *a: subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(REPO), *a], check=True, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
TRACKED = [l for l in git("ls-files").splitlines() if l.strip()]


def stop(msg):
    print(f"STOP: {msg}")
    sys.stdout.flush()
    raise SystemExit(3)


def lines(rel):
    return (REPO / rel).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()


def show(rel, a, b):
    L = lines(rel)
    for i in range(a, b + 1):
        print(f"{rel}:{i}: {L[i - 1]}")


DEFS, TREES = {}, {}


def tree(rel):
    if rel not in TREES:
        TREES[rel] = ast.parse((REPO / rel).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return TREES[rel]


def defs(rel):
    """[(start, end, qualname, kind)] for every function and class (start includes decorators); P3-7b's rule."""
    if rel in DEFS:
        return DEFS[rel]
    out = []

    def visit(node, prefix):
        for ch in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):
            if isinstance(ch, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef)):
                start = min([d.lineno for d in ch.decorator_list] + [ch.lineno])
                q = f"{prefix}.{ch.name}" if prefix else ch.name
                out.append((start, ch.end_lineno, q, "class" if isinstance(ch, ast.ClassDef) else "def"))
                visit(ch, q)
            else:
                visit(ch, prefix)

    visit(tree(rel), "")
    DEFS[rel] = out
    return out


def enclosing(rel, i):
    if not rel.endswith(".py"):
        return "(not Python)"
    inside = [d for d in defs(rel) if d[0] <= i <= d[1]]
    if not inside:
        return "(module level)"
    return min(inside, key=lambda d: d[1] - d[0])[2]


def one_def(rel, qual, kind=None):
    hit = [d for d in defs(rel) if d[2] == qual and (kind is None or d[3] == kind)]
    if len(hit) != 1:
        stop(f"{rel}: {qual} matches {len(hit)} definitions")
    return hit[0]


def class_node(rel, name):
    hit = [n for n in tree(rel).body if isinstance(n, ast.ClassDef) and n.name == name]
    if len(hit) != 1:
        stop(f"{rel}: class {name} matches {len(hit)} top-level definitions")
    return hit[0]


def span(node):
    return min([d.lineno for d in getattr(node, "decorator_list", [])] + [node.lineno]), node.end_lineno


def grep(files, keys, label, nocase=False):
    print(f"keys: {list(keys)}{' (case-insensitive)' if nocase else ''}")
    n = 0
    for rel in files:
        try:
            L = lines(rel)
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            print(f"{rel}: not UTF-8 text, not searched")
            continue
        for i, l in enumerate(L, 1):
            hay = l.lower() if nocase else l
            if any((k.lower() if nocase else k) in hay for k in keys):
                n += 1
                print(f"{rel}:{i}: [{enclosing(rel, i)}] {l}")
    print(f"{label}: {n} lines")


def full(rel):
    b = (REPO / rel).read_bytes()
    n = len(b.decode("utf-8").splitlines())
    print(f"== {rel}  sha256 {hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()}  ({n} lines) ==")
    show(rel, 1, n)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- b.1
print("=== b.1 In full ===")
for rel in ("tests/test_docs_d3.py", "tests/test_io.py", "tests/test_guard_p46.py"):
    full(rel)
    print()
rel = "tests/test_blind.py"
n = len(lines(rel))
print(f"== {rel}, from line 162 to its end ({n}) ==")
show(rel, 162, n)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- b.2
print()
print("=== b.2 Test functions, in full; and their classes' decorators, class-level statements, set-up and tear-down "
      "methods and method list ===")
B2 = [("tests/test_calendar.py", "TestConfirmationCalendar.test_mapping_rule_matches_panel_flags_on_dev"),
      ("tests/test_fctests_c5.py", "TestPairing.test_guard_runs_first"),
      ("tests/test_forecast_c1.py", "TestWalkForward.test_allowlist_guards_and_determinism"),
      ("tests/test_importance_registry_c6.py", "TestRegistry.test_refusals_and_paths"),
      ("tests/test_inference_b1.py", "TestDesign.test_refusals"),
      ("tests/test_inference_b1.py", "TestDesign.test_entry_points_recheck_provenance"),
      ("tests/test_inference_b2.py", "TestB2Blinding.test_forged_provenance_refused"),
      ("tests/test_inference_b3.py", "TestB3Blinding.test_entry_points_refuse_real"),
      ("tests/test_p3_sealed_run.py", "TestSeedsAndLogs.test_8_log_checks"),
      ("tests/test_p3_sealed_run.py", "TestP32d.test_10_r1_log_check_and_logged_panel"),
      ("tests/test_sim_b5.py", "TestSealedFitMechanics.test_real_fit_is_opt_in"),
      ("tests/test_sim_b5.py", "TestSealedFitMechanics.test_refusals_and_guard_intact")]
for rel, q in B2:
    a, b, _, _ = one_def(rel, q, "def")
    print(f"-- {rel}: {q}, lines {a} to {b}")
    show(rel, a, b)
seen = []
for rel, q in B2:
    cls = q.split(".")[0]
    if (rel, cls) in seen:
        continue
    seen.append((rel, cls))
    node = class_node(rel, cls)
    a, b = span(node)
    print(f"-- {rel}: class {cls}, lines {a} to {b}")
    print(f"   decorators: {len(node.decorator_list)}")
    for d in node.decorator_list:
        show(rel, d.lineno, d.end_lineno)
    other = [s for s in node.body if not isinstance(s, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))]
    print(f"   class-level statements other than function definitions: {len(other)}")
    for s in other:
        show(rel, *span(s))
    meths = [s for s in node.body if isinstance(s, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))]
    for m in meths:
        if m.name in ("setUp", "setUpClass", "tearDown", "tearDownClass"):
            print(f"   {m.name}, lines {span(m)[0]} to {span(m)[1]}:")
            show(rel, *span(m))
    print(f"   set-up and tear-down methods present: {[m.name for m in meths if m.name in ('setUp', 'setUpClass', 'tearDown', 'tearDownClass')]}")
    print(f"   methods ({len(meths)}): " + "; ".join(f"{m.name} {span(m)[0]}-{span(m)[1]}" for m in meths))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- b.3
print()
print("=== b.3 Classes, in full ===")
B3 = [("tests/test_lookahead.py", "TestLookAheadRealDev"), ("tests/test_sim_b5.py", "TestRealDev"),
      ("tests/test_targets.py", "TestRealDev")]
for rel, cls in B3:

    a, b = span(node)
    print(f"-- {rel}: class {cls}, lines {a} to {b}")
    show(rel, a, b)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- b.4
print()
print("=== b.4 Module-level statements other than class definitions, for each file of b.2 and b.3 ===")
files = []
for rel, _ in B2 + B3:
    if rel not in files:
        files.append(rel)
for rel in files:
    stmts = [s for s in tree(rel).body if not isinstance(s, ast.ClassDef)]
    print(f"-- {rel}: {len(stmts)} statements")
    for s in stmts:
        a, b = span(s)
        print(f"   lines {a} to {b}:")
        show(rel, a, b)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- b.5
print()
print("=== b.5 Guard and loader names in tracked .py files under ofilt/ and tools/ ===")
LIBPY = [p for p in TRACKED if p.startswith(("ofilt/", "tools/")) and p.endswith(".py")]
grep(LIBPY, ("require_unblinded(", "_tag_ok(", "_check_access(", "check_access(", "is_ancestor_tag(", "REQUIRED_TAGS",
             "SPEC_TAG", "PREREG_TAG", "CONFIRM_TAG", "load_confirm(", "load_dev("), "b.5")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- b.6
print()
print("=== b.6 Log and registry names in tracked files under ofilt/ and tools/ ===")
LIBALL = [p for p in TRACKED if p.startswith(("ofilt/", "tools/"))]
K6 = ("RUN_REGISTRY", "REAL_PATH", "registry_path", "BLIND_FIT_LOG", "ACCESS_LOG")
grep(LIBALL, K6, "b.6")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- b.7
print()
print("=== b.7 In full, each once ===")
printed = set()


def outer_def(rel, i):
    ds = [d for d in defs(rel) if d[3] == "def" and d[0] <= i <= d[1]]
    return max(ds, key=lambda d: d[1] - d[0]) if ds else None


def show_once(rel, a, b, label):
    key = (rel, a, b)
    if key in printed:
        print(f"-- {label}: {rel} lines {a} to {b} (printed above)")
        return
    printed.add(key)
    print(f"-- {label}: {rel} lines {a} to {b}")
    show(rel, a, b)


print("--- b.7.1 functions in tracked .py files under ofilt/ that contain a call to require_unblinded( (not its "
      "definition) or a line of b.6 (the outermost function containing the line)")
OFPY = [p for p in TRACKED if p.startswith("ofilt/") and p.endswith(".py")]
for rel in OFPY:
    L = lines(rel)
    for i, l in enumerate(L, 1):
        hit = ("require_unblinded(" in l and not l.lstrip().startswith("def require_unblinded(")) or any(k in l for k in K6)
        if hit:
            d = outer_def(rel, i)
            if d is None:
                print(f"   {rel}:{i}: at module level, not in a function: {l.strip()}")
            else:
                show_once(rel, d[0], d[1], f"{d[2]} (contains line {i})")
print("--- b.7.2 the module that defines registry_path: every function and every module-level assignment")
mods = [p for p in LIBPY if any(isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)) and n.name == "registry_path"
                                for n in ast.walk(tree(p)))]
print(f"modules defining registry_path: {mods}")
if len(mods) != 1:
    stop(f"registry_path is defined in {len(mods)} modules")
rel = mods[0]
for n in tree(rel).body:
    if isinstance(n, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign, ast.AugAssign)):
        show_once(rel, *span(n), "module-level assignment")
fdefs = [d for d in defs(rel) if d[3] == "def"]
for d in fdefs:                                               # functions not nested inside another function
    if not any(e != d and e[0] <= d[0] and d[1] <= e[1] for e in fdefs):
        show_once(rel, d[0], d[1], d[2])
print("--- b.7.3 ofilt/drift/io.py: _log_access and every module-level assignment")
rel = "ofilt/drift/io.py"
a, b, q, _ = one_def(rel, "_log_access", "def")
show_once(rel, a, b, q)
for n in tree(rel).body:
    if isinstance(n, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign, ast.AugAssign)):
        show_once(rel, *span(n), "module-level assignment")
print("--- b.7.4 the module that defines is_ancestor_tag, in full")
mods = [p for p in LIBPY if any(isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)) and n.name == "is_ancestor_tag"

-- resample_dates (contains line 116): ofilt/drift/calib.py lines 111 to 129

ofilt/drift/calib.py:112:     """Stationary bootstrap of whole dates: the frame's T unique dates (sorted) are drawn to length T with
ofilt/drift/calib.py:113:     sim.stationary_bootstrap_positions(T, mean_block, rng, n_out=T); each drawn date's rows are copied in drawn order
ofilt/drift/calib.py:114:     and the dates are relabelled to a consecutive synthetic business-day calendar from sim.SIM_CALENDAR_START, the
ofilt/drift/calib.py:115:     mechanism of DevCalibrator.draw (sim.py:225-230). The provenance is the input's (synthetic stays synthetic)."""
ofilt/drift/calib.py:116:     prov = blind.require_unblinded(frame)
ofilt/drift/calib.py:117:     f = frame.sort_values(["date", "ticker"], kind="mergesort")
ofilt/drift/calib.py:118:     codes = np.unique(f["date"].to_numpy(dtype="datetime64[ns]"), return_inverse=True)[1]
ofilt/drift/calib.py:119:     bounds = np.r_[0, np.cumsum(np.bincount(codes))]
ofilt/drift/calib.py:120:     T = len(bounds) - 1
ofilt/drift/calib.py:121:     pos = _sim.stationary_bootstrap_positions(T, mean_block, rng, n_out=T)
ofilt/drift/calib.py:122:     lens = np.diff(bounds)[pos]
ofilt/drift/calib.py:123:     offs = np.r_[0, np.cumsum(lens)[:-1]]
ofilt/drift/calib.py:124:     src = np.repeat(bounds[pos] - offs, lens) + np.arange(int(lens.sum()))
ofilt/drift/calib.py:125:     out = f.iloc[src].reset_index(drop=True)
ofilt/drift/calib.py:126:     cal = pd.bdate_range(_sim.SIM_CALENDAR_START, periods=T)
ofilt/drift/calib.py:127:     out["date"] = np.repeat(cal.to_numpy(), lens)

-- pooled_ols (contains line 151): ofilt/drift/inference.py lines 150 to 155
ofilt/drift/inference.py:150: def pooled_ols(design: Design) -> OLSFit:
ofilt/drift/inference.py:151:     blind.require_unblinded(provenance=design.provenance, arrays={"y": design.y})
ofilt/drift/inference.py:152:     Q, R = np.linalg.qr(design.X, mode="reduced")
ofilt/drift/inference.py:153:     coef = linalg.solve_triangular(R, Q.T @ design.y)
ofilt/drift/inference.py:154:     Rinv = linalg.solve_triangular(R, np.eye(R.shape[0]))
ofilt/drift/inference.py:155:     return OLSFit(design, coef, design.y - design.X @ coef, Rinv @ Rinv.T)

ofilt/drift/inference.py:179:     """DK covariance of the OLS coefficients and the lag used."""

tools/checkpoint.sh:1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
tools/checkpoint.sh:2: # usage: bash tools/checkpoint.sh "<item id>" ["<extra commit-message paragraph>" ...]
tools/checkpoint.sh:3: # Pass 2 checkpoint (P2-0e, P51): runs the test suite, then commits as "checkpoint: <item id>".
tools/checkpoint.sh:4: # Portable: the repo root comes from git, OFILT_DATA_DIR from the environment, python from the
tools/checkpoint.sh:5: # active venv. No mirroring, no tarball. Refuses to checkpoint on any test failure.
tools/checkpoint.sh:6: # The real-data sealed fit is opt-in via the OFILT_RUN_SEALED variable; checkpoints unset it.
tools/checkpoint.sh:7: set -uo pipefail
tools/checkpoint.sh:8:

tools/checkpoint.sh:11: shift
tools/checkpoint.sh:12: root="$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" || { echo "not inside a git repository; not checkpointing"; exit 2; }
tools/checkpoint.sh:13: cd "$root" || exit 2
tools/checkpoint.sh:14:

tools/checkpoint.sh:16: if [ -z "${VIRTUAL_ENV:-}" ]; then echo "VIRTUAL_ENV is unset (activate the venv); not checkpointing"; exit 2; fi
tools/checkpoint.sh:17: py="$(command -v python || true)"

tools/checkpoint.sh:19:     echo "python is '$py', not '$VIRTUAL_ENV/bin/python'; not checkpointing"; exit 2
tools/checkpoint.sh:20: fi

tools/checkpoint.sh:28: diff_before="$(git diff --binary | shasum -a 256 | cut -d' ' -f1)"
tools/checkpoint.sh:29: echo "before suite: git diff --binary sha256 $diff_before"

--- c.2 The suite in the clone, verbose (/tmp/p3_8a_suite.py; log /tmp/p3_8a_suite_v.log, not printed) ---

"""P3-8a step c.2: the suite in the scratch clone, verbose (env -u OFILT_RUN_SEALED, OFILT_DATA_DIR = DATA,
python -m unittest discover -s tests -v, from the clone's root; no tag, no commit). The log is saved to
/tmp/p3_8a_suite_v.log; only these are printed: the Ran line and the result line, every skipped test with its reason
verbatim, and the number of tests in each test module with their sum. No other line of the log is printed.
usage: python /tmp/p3_8a_suite.py /tmp/p3_8a_clone"""
import collections
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

clone = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()
if clone != Path("/tmp/p3_8a_clone").resolve():
    raise SystemExit(f"REFUSED: {clone} is not the /tmp/p3_8a_clone scratch clone")
env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k != "OFILT_RUN_SEALED"}
env["OFILT_DATA_DIR"] = os.path.expanduser("~/Desktop/FX_Project/data")
which = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", "import ofilt; print(ofilt.__file__)"], cwd=clone, env=env,
                       capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
under = Path(which).resolve().is_relative_to(clone)
print(f"python {sys.executable}; ofilt imported from {which}; under the clone: {under}")
if not under:
    raise SystemExit("STOP: ofilt does not import from the clone")
log = Path("/tmp/p3_8a_suite_v.log")
with open(log, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
    rc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "unittest", "discover", "-s", "tests", "-v"], cwd=clone, env=env,
                        stdout=fh, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT).returncode
L = log.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
ran = [l for l in L if re.match(r"^Ran \d+ tests? in [0-9.]+s$", l)]
res = [l for l in L if re.match(r"^(OK|FAILED)( \(.*\))?$", l)]
print(f"exit {rc}")
print(f"Ran line: {ran}")
print(f"result line: {res}")
ids = []                                                       # (line index, method, test id)
for i, l in enumerate(L):
    m = re.match(r"^(\w+) \(([\w.]+)\)", l)
    if m and m.group(2).endswith("." + m.group(1)):
        ids.append((i, m.group(1), m.group(2)))
print("skipped tests, with their reasons (verbatim):")
nskip = 0
for i, meth, tid in ids:
    for l in L[i:i + 2]:
        s = re.search(r" \.\.\. skipped (.*)$", l) or re.match(r"^skipped (.*)$", l)
        if s:
            nskip += 1
            print(f"  {tid}: skipped {s.group(1)}")
            break
per = collections.Counter(tid.rsplit(".", 2)[0] for _, _, tid in ids)
print("tests per module:")
for mod in sorted(per):
    print(f"  {mod}: {per[mod]}")
total = sum(per.values())
nran = int(re.match(r"^Ran (\d+)", ran[-1]).group(1)) if ran else None
print(f"sum: {total}; Ran count: {nran}; equal: {total == nran}; skipped found: {nskip}")
fails = [l for l in L if re.match(r"^(FAIL|ERROR): ", l)]
if fails:
    print("failures and errors:")
    for l in fails:
        print(f"  {l}")
ok = rc == 0 and ran and ran[-1].startswith("Ran 285 tests") and res and res[-1] == "OK (skipped=6)" and total == nran
print("SUITE CHECK:", bool(ok))
raise SystemExit(0 if ok else 1)

--- c.2, the parse rerun (rerun-once rule; /tmp/p3_8a_suite_parse.py on the saved log; the suite is not rerun) ---
The first parse (above) listed 10 skipped tests where the result line says skipped=6: it took a test's result from its own line or the next line, so four tests whose result was not on their own line took the next test's "skipped" (each listed just before a true skip). The suite itself ran once, correctly (exit 0, Ran 285, OK (skipped=6), module sum 285). The fixed rule takes the next line only if it is not another test's line, and counts every result kind.
Script in full:
<<<<<<<<
"""P3-8a step c.2, the parse rerun (rerun-once rule): the saved verbose log /tmp/p3_8a_suite_v.log, parsed again with
a fixed rule; the suite is not rerun. The first parse (/tmp/p3_8a_suite.py) took a test's result from its own line or
the next line, so a test whose result was not on its own line could take the next test's "skipped". Here a test's
result is on its own line, or on the next line only if that line is not another test's line. Only these are printed:
the Ran and result lines, every skipped test with its reason verbatim, the count of each result kind, and the number of
tests in each module with their sum. No other line of the log is printed."""
import collections
import re
from pathlib import Path

L = Path("/tmp/p3_8a_suite_v.log").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
ran = [l for l in L if re.match(r"^Ran \d+ tests? in [0-9.]+s$", l)]
res = [l for l in L if re.match(r"^(OK|FAILED)( \(.*\))?$", l)]
print(f"Ran line: {ran}")
print(f"result line: {res}")
IDRX = re.compile(r"^(\w+) \(([\w.]+)\)")
RESRX = re.compile(r" \.\.\. (ok|skipped .*|FAIL|ERROR|expected failure|unexpected success)$")


def is_id(l):
    m = IDRX.match(l)
    return bool(m and m.group(2).endswith("." + m.group(1)))


ids = [(i, IDRX.match(l).group(2)) for i, l in enumerate(L) if is_id(l)]
kinds, skipped, unknown = collections.Counter(), [], []
for i, tid in ids:
    r = RESRX.search(L[i])
    if not r and i + 1 < len(L) and not is_id(L[i + 1]):
        r = RESRX.search(L[i + 1])
    if not r:
        unknown.append(tid)
        continue
    kind = r.group(1).split(" ")[0]
    kinds[kind] += 1
    if kind == "skipped":
        skipped.append((tid, r.group(1)))
print("skipped tests, with their reasons (verbatim):")
for tid, s in skipped:
    print(f"  {tid}: {s}")
print(f"result kinds: {dict(kinds)}; tests with no result found: {unknown}")
per = collections.Counter(tid.rsplit(".", 2)[0] for _, tid in ids)
print("tests per module:")
for mod in sorted(per):
    print(f"  {mod}: {per[mod]}")
total = sum(per.values())
nran = int(re.match(r"^Ran (\d+)", ran[-1]).group(1)) if ran else None
nskip_res = int(re.search(r"skipped=(\d+)", res[-1]).group(1)) if res and "skipped=" in res[-1] else 0
print(f"sum: {total}; Ran count: {nran}; equal: {total == nran}")
print(f"skipped found: {len(skipped)}; the result line's skipped count: {nskip_res}; equal: {len(skipped) == nskip_res}")
print(f"results found for every test: {not unknown and sum(kinds.values()) == total}")
ok = (ran and ran[-1].startswith("Ran 285 tests") and res and res[-1] == "OK (skipped=6)" and total == nran
      and len(skipped) == nskip_res == 6 and not unknown and sum(kinds.values()) == total)
print("PARSE CHECK:", bool(ok))
raise SystemExit(0 if ok else 1)

"""P3-8a step d: the copy of the P3-7b report. Expected lines are read by script from the saved P3-8a message
(/tmp/p3_8a_msg.txt, section d), never typed."""
import hashlib
import os
import re
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(os.path.expanduser("~/Desktop/FX_Project/ofilt_pass1"))
MSG = Path("/tmp/p3_8a_msg.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
sec = MSG.split("## d. The reports trail")[1].split("## e.")[0]
sha = lambda p: hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
ok = True


def chk(label, cond):
    global ok
    ok &= bool(cond)
    print(f"{label}: {bool(cond)}")


src, dst = Path("/tmp/p3_7b.txt"), REPO / "reports" / "P3_reports" / "p3_7b.txt"
a, b = sha(dst), sha(src)
print(f"copy   {dst.relative_to(REPO)} sha256 {a}")
print(f"source {src} sha256 {b}")
chk("p3_7b.txt: copy equals source", a == b)
first = re.search(r"first two lines are `([^`]+)` and `([^`]+)`", sec).groups()
last = re.search(r"last two non-empty lines are `([^`]+)` and `([^`]+)`", sec).groups()
line = re.search(r"it contains, once, the line `([^`]+)`", sec).group(1)
lines = dst.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
nonempty = [l for l in lines if l.strip()]
chk(f"p3_7b.txt: first two lines {lines[:2]} equal {list(first)}", tuple(lines[:2]) == first)
chk(f"p3_7b.txt: last two non-empty lines {nonempty[-2:]} equal {list(last)}", tuple(nonempty[-2:]) == last)
n = sum(l == line for l in lines)
chk(f"p3_7b.txt: the line '{line}' occurs exactly once ({n})", n == 1)
print("D CHECK:", ok)
raise SystemExit(0 if ok else 1)

"""P3-8a step e: targeted PASS3_STATUS.md edits. Copied verbatim from the saved P3-8a message (/tmp/p3_8a_msg.txt,
section e): the Decision-log block, P3-7b's new status and the Next action. Built by script: the P3-8a row and the Test
results (from the report so far, /tmp/p3_8a.txt). Each anchor must occur exactly once. Nothing is written unless every
step succeeds."""
import os
import re
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(os.path.expanduser("~/Desktop/FX_Project/ofilt_pass1"))
F = REPO / "PASS3_STATUS.md"
MSG = Path("/tmp/p3_8a_msg.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
sec_e = MSG.split("## e. Records (PASS3_STATUS.md)")[1].split("## f.")[0]
REP = Path("/tmp/p3_8a.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
s = F.read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def sub(a, b):
    global s
    n = s.count(a)
    if n != 1:
        raise SystemExit(f"anchor found {n} times: {a[:80]!r}")
    s = s.replace(a, b)


def part(start, end):
    a = REP.index("\n" + start)
    return REP[a:REP.index("\n" + end, a + 1)]


def item(n):
    return re.split(r"^\d+\. \*\*", sec_e, flags=re.M)[n]


checks = lambda t: [l for l in t.splitlines() if re.match(r"^\S.*: (True|False)$", l)]

# 1. Decision log
block = [l for l in item(1).splitlines() if l.startswith(">")]
print(f"Decision-log block: {len(block)} lines, copied from the P3-8a message's section e.1")
if len(block) != 5:
    raise SystemExit("the block is not 5 lines")
sub("\n\n## Test results\n", "\n\n" + "\n".join(block) + "\n\n## Test results\n")

# the report's facts
d = part("--- d. ", "--- e. ").split("python /tmp/p3_8a_d.py\n", 1)[1] if "\n--- e. " in REP else \
    REP[REP.index("\n--- d. "):].split("python /tmp/p3_8a_d.py\n", 1)[1]
d_lines = [l for l in d.splitlines() if l.startswith(("copy ", "source ")) or re.match(r"^p3_7b\.txt: .*: (True|False)$", l)]
pr = REP[REP.index("\n--- c.2, the parse rerun"):].split("python /tmp/p3_8a_suite_parse.py\n", 1)[1]
pr = pr[:pr.index("\n--- c.3 ")]
ran = re.search(r"^Ran line: \['(Ran \d+ tests in [\d.]+s)'\]$", pr, re.M).group(1)
res = re.search(r"^result line: \['([^']+)'\]$", pr, re.M).group(1)
kinds = re.search(r"^result kinds: (\{.*\}); tests with no result found: \[\]$", pr, re.M).group(1)
skips = re.findall(r"^  (\S+): (skipped .*)$", pr, re.M)
nmods = len(re.findall(r"^  test_\w+: \d+$", pr, re.M))
b = REP[REP.index("\n--- b. "):REP.index("\n--- c.1 ")]
n13 = re.search(r"^count: (\d+)$", b, re.M).group(1)
if len(skips) != 6:
    raise SystemExit(f"{len(skips)} skipped tests read from the parse rerun, not 6")

# 2. Progress
p7b = "| P3-7b P3-7a and P3-7a2 accepted; the final Pass 3 records, rehearsed in a scratch clone | done |"
status = re.search(r"P3-7b's status becomes `([^`]+)`", item(2)).group(1)
sub(p7b, p7b.replace("| done |", f"| {status} |"))
title = "P3-8a " + re.search(r"^# Pass 3, item P3-8a: (.+?) \(supervisor message", MSG, re.M).group(1)
notes = ("P3-7b acceptance, the tag-aware tests' routing and the note on the fifth supervisor chat recorded. The P3-7b "
         "report copied to `reports/P3_reports/`. Read-only facts for P3-8b (step b, in the report): the tag and guard "
         "tests, the tests that pass dev_real frames to an entry point and their classes, the code they reach, the "
         "skips, the data paths, `tools/checkpoint.sh`, git, and the SHA-256 of every tracked file under `tests/`, "
         f"`ofilt/` and `tools/` ({n13}). The suite in a scratch clone, verbose: `{ran.split(' in ')[0]}`, `{res}`, by "
         f"module ({nmods} modules); the six skips named. No tag created or deleted; `tools/p3_sealed_run.py` run only "
         "with `--check-config`")
row8a = f"| {title} | in progress | {notes} |"
i = s.index(p7b.replace("| done |", f"| {status} |"))
j = s.index("\n", i)
if not s[j + 1:].startswith("| Pass 3 | in progress | |"):
    raise SystemExit("the P3-7b row is not followed by the Pass 3 row")
s = s[:j + 1] + row8a + "\n" + s[j + 1:]

# 3. Next action
na = re.search(r'becomes: "([^"]+)"', item(3)).group(1)
cur = s.split("## Next action\n\n")[1].split("\n\n## Decision log")[0]
if "\n" in cur:
    raise SystemExit("the Next action is not one line")
sub("## Next action\n\n" + cur + "\n\n## Decision log", "## Next action\n\n" + na + "\n\n## Decision log")

# 4. Test results
TR = ["**P3-8a:**",
      "- The report copy (step d; bash, `cp -p /tmp/p3_7b.txt reports/P3_reports/p3_7b.txt`), checked by `/tmp/p3_8a_d.py`:",
      *[f"  - {l}" for l in d_lines],
      "- The suite in a scratch clone, verbose (`/tmp/p3_8a_suite.py`; no tag, no commit; the log parsed again by "

      "- The full suite ran inside the P3-8a checkpoint; its result is in the P3-8a report (`/tmp/p3_8a.txt`)."]
sub("\n\n## Test changes\n", "\n\n" + "\n".join(TR) + "\n\n## Test changes\n")

# 5. Blinding attestation
BA = ["- **P3-8a (supervisor message of 2026-10-07)**, by category:",
      "  - Records: document edits to PASS3_STATUS.md. The report copy (`reports/P3_reports/p3_7b.txt`, byte for byte).",
      "  - No script of this item opened a data file. The code of step b (tracked files under `tests/`, `ofilt/` and "

      "  - The two suites (in a scratch clone, verbose, and inside the checkpoint) read the dev panel only through the "
      "tests' structural and target-only reads; only the Ran and result lines, the skips and the per-module counts of "
      "the clone's log were printed.",
      "  - No tag was created, moved or deleted, in REPO or in the clone. `tools/p3_sealed_run.py` ran only with "
      "`--check-config`. No viewer was used. RAW and OUT: untouched. `OFILT_RUN_SEALED=1` was never set; every Python "
      "command ran in the venv under `env -u OFILT_RUN_SEALED`. BLIND_FIT_LOG has 7 dev_real lines, and "
      "RUN_REGISTRY.jsonl is 0 bytes."]
sub("\n\n## Deviations\n", "\n" + "\n".join(BA) + "\n\n## Deviations\n")

# 6. Deviations
OWN = ["- P3-8a c.2, the parse: the first parse of the clone's verbose log (`/tmp/p3_8a_suite.py`) listed 10 skipped "
       "tests where the result line says skipped=6. It took a test's result from its own line or the next one, so four "
       "tests whose result was not on their own line took the next test's \"skipped\". Under the rerun-once rule the "
       "parse was fixed (the next line is read only if it is not another test's line) and run once more on the saved "

       "correctly; both parses are in the report.",
       "- P3-8a b, choices the item left open: in b.7, \"every function that contains\" a line is read as the outermost "

       "methods, not nested functions separately (one edit before the script's only run); b.1 also prints each file's "
       "SHA-256 and line count."]
if not s.endswith("\n"):
    raise SystemExit("PASS3_STATUS.md does not end with a newline")
s = s + "\n".join(OWN) + "\n"
F.write_text(s, encoding="utf-8")

t = F.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

"""P3-8a step e.7, the records rule in its post-switch form (DQ6). The six reserved prefixes are read from the saved
P3-8a message's records-rule paragraph, and the decision block's checkpoint from its step e.7. Checks: lines added to
PASS3_STATUS.md since HEAD that begin with a reserved prefix (must be 0); lines in the file beginning with each prefix
(exactly 1 each); the checkpoint is HEAD and appears exactly once in the added Decision-log lines. Also BLIND_FIT_LOG's
dev_real count and RUN_REGISTRY.jsonl's size. The configuration check is /tmp/p3_8a_cfg.py."""
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(os.path.expanduser("~/Desktop/FX_Project/ofilt_pass1"))
MSG = Path("/tmp/p3_8a_msg.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
para = MSG.split("**The records rule, post-switch form (DQ6).**")[1].split("**Files that may change")[0]
PREFIXES = re.findall(r"`([^`]+)`", para.split("one of the six reserved prefixes")[1].split(", and each of them")[0])
git = lambda *a: subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(REPO), *a], check=True, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
ok = True


def chk(label, cond):
    global ok
    ok &= bool(cond)
    print(f"{label}: {bool(cond)}")


print(f"reserved prefixes ({len(PREFIXES)}): {PREFIXES}")
chk("six prefixes", len(PREFIXES) == 6)
d = git("diff", "-U0", "HEAD", "--", "PASS3_STATUS.md")
added = [l[1:] for l in d.splitlines() if l.startswith("+") and not l.startswith("+++")]
n_added = sum(any(l.startswith(p) for p in PREFIXES) for l in added)
chk(f"lines added since HEAD: {len(added)}; beginning with a reserved prefix: {n_added} (must be 0)", n_added == 0)
lines = (REPO / "PASS3_STATUS.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
for p in PREFIXES:
    hits = [i + 1 for i, l in enumerate(lines) if l.startswith(p)]
    chk(f"  lines beginning with {p!r}: {len(hits)} (line {hits})", len(hits) == 1)
sec7 = MSG.split("7. **Checks**")[1].split("## f.")[0]
cp = re.search(r"the decision block's checkpoint `([0-9a-f]{40})` is HEAD", sec7).group(1)
head = git("rev-parse", "HEAD").strip()
chk(f"the decision block's checkpoint {cp} is HEAD {head}", cp == head)
dl = [l for l in added if l.startswith(">")]
n = sum(cp in l for l in dl)
chk(f"Decision-log lines added: {len(dl)}; the checkpoint appears in them {n} time(s) (must be 1)", n == 1)
recs = [json.loads(l) for l in (REPO / "BLIND_FIT_LOG").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]
nd = sum(r.get("provenance") == "dev_real" for r in recs)
chk(f"BLIND_FIT_LOG: {len(recs)} lines, {nd} with provenance dev_real (must be 7 and 7)", len(recs) == 7 and nd == 7)
nb = (REPO / "RUN_REGISTRY.jsonl").stat().st_size
chk(f"RUN_REGISTRY.jsonl bytes {nb} is 0", nb == 0)
print("RECORDS RULE:", ok)
raise SystemExit(0 if ok else 1)

"""P3-8a step f.2: the commit's files, compared as a set with the two paths the saved P3-8a message names (section f.2).

import os
import re

want = set(re.findall(r"`([^`]+)`", re.search(r"lists exactly two paths, compared as a set: (.*?)\. The 21", sec).group(1)))
git = lambda *a: subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(REPO), *a], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
ok = True


def chk(label, cond):
    global ok
    ok &= bool(cond)
    print(f"{label}: {bool(cond)}")
